Another idea: Use **IRFFT spectral shaping** to make c_k flat. In Fourier, C = A^2. We want C flat. We can iteratively set `A_new = A * exp(η (log C_target - log |C|)/2)`? Since C coefficients are time-domain; not phase. But A has phase. We can use FFT of c? Actually C(z) coefficients c; its DFT is A(ω)^2. If we want c flat, DFT flat. Current DFT D(ω)=A(ω)^2. We can adjust A by multiplying by sqrt(target_D/current_D) (real positive in frequency) if possible, i.e., adjust frequency magnitude. This is like Fienup but with square. Algorithm:
- Start a.
- Compute C = FFT(a)^2 (complex). Its coefficient (inverse) is c.
- Target C coefficients c_target (e.g., current c clipped to max M? or uniform). Compute D_target = FFT(c_target).
- Update A_new = A * sqrt(D_target / C) (with phase of A, magnitude adjusted). Then a_new=ifft(A_new)[:n], clip nonnegative.
This directly shapes the square spectrum to flatten convolution coefficients. This is a very strong method! Let's detail.
Given A(ω)=∑ a_k e^{-iωk}. C(ω)=A(ω)^2. Its inverse (with appropriate length) is c_k. If we want c to approach target t_k (nonnegative, sum=S^2 fixed), we need C_target(ω)=FFT(t). To adjust A while preserving phase as much as possible, set |A_new| = |A| * sqrt(|C_target|/|C|), phase half? But C_target/D may have phase; we can use log after Fourier? If we simply multiply A by sqrt(D_target/C) in frequency, C_new = A^2 * D_target/C = D_target if exact, but squaring a modified A with modified phase? If multiply A by H, C_new=H^2 C. Need H^2 =D_target/C, choose H=sqrt(D_target/C). That changes phase by half log; okay. Then a_new inverse may be complex and have negative values. Clip/normalize. This is a projection method: constraint in c-domain (target coefficients) and positivity in a-domain. It generalizes Fienup and might converge to flat c. Very promising.

Let's write helpers:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def normalize_sum(a):
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-300: return a; return a/s

def softmax(x):
   xm=x-x.max()
   e=np.exp(xm)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: exp uniform
   return e/s

def linear_conv(a, N=None):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
   A=np.fft.fft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:L]
   return c  # may tiny neg

def score_all(a):
   s=a.sum(); if s<1e-300 return inf
   c=linear_conv(a/s, None) # normalized? Since score scale invariant, normalize once.
   return 2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c))
```
For internal score, normalize to sum1 to avoid large. But if actual eval with clipping maybe scale irrelevant. Good.
```
def score_value_from_a(a): # a already sum1
   c=linear_conv(a); return 2n*max(c)
```
Careful: if a not sum1, normalize inside returns score. If we call many times, repeated normalize. Use internal `score_normalized(a)`.

`conv_and_c(a, N)` returns c. For LSE objective, need a sum1 approx from softmax. No normalize needed. But due to floating, sum exactly1 within eps.

`lse_soft_obj(x, beta, n, N)`:
Potential performance: `np.fft.fft` and `ifft` allocate. We can use `np.fft.rfft` for a? For corr need full. We'll use full.
```
def lse_soft_obj(x, beta, n, N):
    a = softmax(x)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.ifft(C).real[:2*n-1]
    cmax = c.max()
    sh = beta*(c-cmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -500, 500)
    es = np.exp(sh)
    st = es.sum()
    if st<=0: ...
    p = es/st
    L = cmax + np.log(st)/beta
    P = np.fft.fft(p, N)
    corr = np.fft.ifft(A * np.conj(P)).real[:n]
    # Gradient of L: 2*corr. score=2nL, grad_x=a*(4n corr - 4n dot?)
    mean_corr = float(np.dot(a, corr))
    grad = 4.0*n*a*(corr - mean_corr)
    # add reg
    reg=1e-14
    val = 2.0*n*L + reg*float(np.dot(x,x))
    grad = grad + 2*reg*x
    if not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    return float(val), grad
```
Reg on unbounded x may grow if x drift; set small. Could use reg=1e-12? Objective value around1.5, reg*||x||^2: x from log binomial ~ log(1) to log(1e-5) ~ -11, norm^2 maybe 1e4*? ~1e3, reg1e-14 ->1e-11. Fine.
But if x drifts to 1000, reg penalizes. Good.

```
def lbfgs_soft(a0, betas, t_end, base_maxfun=800):
   best_a=normalize(a0); best_sc=score_normalized(best_a)

      # allocate fun proportional to beta importance; maybe 1/(idx+1)
      share = 1.0/(len(betas))
      maxfun = int(max(200, min(8000, total_budget*share*0.5* (1+0.5*(idx==len(betas)//2)))) )
      t0=time.monotonic()
      try res=minimize(...)
      except:
      sc=score_normalized(a)
      if sc<best: update
      x = res.x - res.x.max()
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def subgrad_soft(a0, iters, top_frac=0.1, eta=1e-4, t_end=None):
   a=normalize(a0); n; N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   x=log(a); step=eta; best...
   for it in iters:
      A=fft(a,N); C=A*A; c=ifft(C)[:L].real
      M=c.max(); sc=2nM; update
      # choose weights p: top top_frac lags or all with high beta
      # Use all with beta=50 to smooth? maybe top 100.
      vals = np.sort(c)[::-1]
      k=max(1,int(len(c)*top_frac))
     thresh=np.min(vals[:k])? use p = exp(beta*(c-cmax)) over all.
      p = exp(beta*(c-cmax))
      p /= sum
      P=fft(p,N); corr=ifft(A*conj(P)).real[:n]
      mean=float(a@corr)
      g = 4n*a*(corr-mean)
      norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if norm small break
      # Polyak step: (sc-lower)/norm^2; lower=2n/(2n-1) maybe
      lower=2.0*n/(2*n-1)
      pstep=(sc-lower+1e-15)/(norm*norm+1e-15)
      st=min(step,pstep,1e-3); st=max(st,1e-8)
      # line search
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(10):
         x_new=x - st*g
         a_new=softmax(x_new)
         sc_new=score_normalized(a_new)
         if sc_new < sc:
            x,x_new; a=a_new; accept; break
         st*=0.5
      if not accept: step*=0.5; break after many no accept
      step=dec? maybe step *= 0.999
   return best
```

Implementation details for `spectral_projection`:
```
def spectral_square_project(a0, iters=300, blend=0.5, t_end=None):
    a=normalize(a0); n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L)
    best=a; best_sc=score_norm(a)
    A=np.fft.fft(a,N)
    for it in range(iters):
       C=A*A
       c=np.fft.ifft(C).real[:L]
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       c_sum=c.sum()
       if c_sum<=0: break
       c=c/c_sum
       # target flatten: t = (1-blend)*c + blend*(1/L)
       # Optionally also reduce max: use gamma = 1 - blend*0.5? 
       target = (1.0-blend)*c + blend/L
       target /= target.sum()
       Dt = np.fft.fft(target,N)
       mask = np.abs(C)>1e-30*np.max(np.abs(C))
       H=np.ones_like(C)
       H[mask]=np.sqrt(Dt[mask]/C[mask])
       # For zero C, use H = sqrt(Dt/max(C))? maybe.
       maxC=np.max(np.abs(C))+1e-300
       H[~mask]=np.sqrt(Dt[~mask]/maxC)
       A_new=A*H
       # enforce hermitian
       idx=np.arange(N)
       A_sym=0.5*(A_new + np.conj(A_new[(N-idx)%N]))
       a_new=np.fft.ifft(A_sym).real[:n]
       a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
       s=a_new.sum()
       if s<1e-12:
           a_new=a; break
       a_new/=s
       sc=score_norm(a_new)
       if sc<best_sc: best=a_new; best_sc=sc
       A=np.fft.fft(a_new,N)
       if time... break
    return best,best_sc
```
Need check `C=A*A` may not be Hermitian due to rounding but fine. `Dt` FFT of target real not Hermitian? It should be close; ratio phase. `H` complex. `A_sym` enforces real output.
Potential issue: if target sum=1, `Dt` magnitude at DC =1, C[0]=|A0|^2=1 (since sum a=1). Good.
Blend values: start 0.2 to 0.7. Also use gamma flattening: target = c**gamma normalized, gamma=0.7,0.5,0.3. Maybe better than flat. We can implement `target_from_c(c, mode, param)`.

- 'clip': c normalized, then reduce top q: `t=c.copy(); thresh=np.quantile(t, q); t[t>thresh]=thresh; t/=sum`.
Use variants.

`binomial_sequence(n)`:
```
def binomial_sequence(n):
   a=np.ones(n)
   if n>1:
      val=1.0
      a[0]=1.0
      for k in range(1,n):
         val *= (k - 0.5) / (2.0*k)
         a[k]=val
   return normalize_sum(a)
```
But normalized sum; for internal maybe sum1. To preserve relative, okay.

```
def tapered_binomial(n, alpha, p):
   b=binomial_unnorm(n)
   i=np.arange(n,float)
   mask=1.0 - alpha*(i/n)**p
   a=b*np.maximum(mask,0.0)
   return normalize_sum(a)
```

```
def linear_conv(a, N=None):
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1
  if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
  return c
```
For real and convolution, rfft good. Use in score.

Implement `conv_corr(a,p,N,n)` helper top-level:
```
def convolution_and_corr(a,p,N,n):
   L=2*n-1
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,N)
   if n>1: corr = conv[2*n-2:n-2:-1].copy()
   else: corr = np.array([float(np.dot(a,p[:1]))?])
   return c, corr
```

Detailed functions:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full_like(a, 1.0/len(a)) if a.size else np.array([1.0])
    return a / s
```

`score_normalized(a)`:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    if n==1: return 2.0
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    # due to roundoff, set small neg to 0
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if mx <= 0: mx=0
    return 2.0 * n * mx
```
Since score=2n max c =2 max(n*c). Good.

`lse_soft_obj(x,beta,n,N)`:
```
def lse_soft_obj(x, beta, n, N):
    a = softmax(x)
    L=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    # score scale
    d = n*c
    dmax = float(d.max())
    sh = beta*(d - dmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -500, 500)
    es = np.exp(sh)
    st = float(es.sum())
    if st <= 0 or not np.isfinite(st):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    p = es/st
    Lval = dmax + float(np.log(st)/beta)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    if n == 1:
       corr = np.array([float(a[0]*p[0])])
    else:
       corr = conv[2*n-2:n-2:-1].copy()
    mean_corr = float(np.dot(a, corr))
    grad_a = 4.0 * n * (corr - mean_corr)  # Wait grad_x = a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a)) but grad_a already? Let's step:
       # score =2L; dL/da = n*2corr? Wait d=n*c, dL/da = ∑p ∂d/∂a = n*2corr.
       # score grad_a = 2 * n*2corr =4n corr.
       # grad_x = a*(grad_a - dot(a, grad_a))
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - mean_grad)
    # reg
    reg=1e-14
    val = 2.0*Lval + reg*float(np.dot(x,x))
    grad_x = grad_x + 2.0*reg*x
    if not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    return float(val), grad_x
```
Correct? Let's verify derivative of L wrt a for d: d_k = n c_k. ∂d/∂a_j = n ∂c = 2n corr[j]. p weights sum1, dL/da = ∑p 2n corr =2n corr. Wait earlier I said n*2corr =2n corr. Yes. Score=2L, grad_a=4n corr. Good. Then grad_x = a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a)). If corr mean dot, grad_a mean =4n dot(a,corr). Thus grad_x =4n a*(corr - dot). Good.
But if LSE uses L = (1/β) logsum, p normalized, derivative independent of β. Good.

In value, `2.0*Lval` approximates score. But note if x changes scale? softmax sum1, score exact =2 max d. Good.

Reg: If x norm huge, val maybe dominates and gradient pulls to 0. okay.

Potential issue: `P = rfft(p[::-1], N)` each eval; p length L, reversed. For large beta, p sparse, rfft still full. Fine.

`run_lbfgs_soft(a0, betas, t_end, maxfun_base=1500)`:
```
def run_lbfgs_soft(a0, betas, t_end, maxfun_base=1500):
    a0=normalize_sum(a0)
    n=a0.size
    if n<=1: return a0,2.0
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_sc=score_normalized(a0)
    x=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); x-=x.max()
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    total_betas=len(betas)
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() > t_end - 5: break
        # maxfun: allocate more to middle; simple use 1500 + 500*idx? Need not too high.
        maxfun = max(300, int(maxfun_base * (1.0 + 0.2*idx)))
        maxiter = int(maxfun)
        try:
           res = minimize(lse_soft_obj, x, args=(float(beta), n, N), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                          options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-17, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':50})
           x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
           a = softmax(x)
           sc = score_normalized(a)
           if sc < best_sc: best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
           x = x - x.max()
        except Exception:
           pass
        print? We can print inside helper? Yes.
    return best_a,best_sc
```
But `maxiter` as maxfun*? L-BFGS `maxiter` linesearch cycles, `maxfun` function evals. Set maxiter=maxfun maybe. If maxfun 1500, time okay. Total betas 8 ->12k evals. Might be okay. We can adjust after considering 2400s; if each eval 0.02s -> 240s. Could do more. Set maxfun_base=4000 for n<=12000? But 8*4000=32k*0.05=1600s. Need leave. We can adapt to n: if n>15000, smaller. Use `maxfun_base = max(500, int(2000000 / n))`? For n=10000, 200; too small. Let's set based on time: We want total fun maybe 10000-20000. betas len 8 ->2000 each. We'll use maxfun_base=2500.

` spectral_square_project(a0, t_end, iters=500, blend=0.5, mode='conv')`:
Need mode select target.
```
def make_target(c, mode, param):
   L=len(c)
   s=c.sum()
   c=c/s if s>0 else np.full(L,1/L)
   if mode == 'conv':
      return (1-param)*c + param*np.full(L,1.0/L)
   elif mode == 'pow':
      g=c**param
      gsum=g.sum(); return g/gsum if gsum>0 else c
   elif mode == 'clip': # param=q quantile threshold level? Use reduce top to certain quantile of active
      # param is fraction of mass to shift? target = c clipped at quantile 1-param
      thresh = np.quantile(c, 1.0-param)
      t=np.minimum(c, thresh)
      # redistribute reduction evenly to non-peak? simply normalize
      tsum=t.sum()
      return t/tsum if tsum>0 else c
   else: return c
```
No lambda. `np.quantile` okay.
For 'clip', if threshold low, tsum<1, normalizing raises peaks back? If t below threshold normalized may still max threshold/tsum > threshold. Need instead add reduction to lows. Better target: reduce peaks to threshold, add excess uniformly to all to keep sum. 
```
t=c.copy()
thresh=np.quantile(c, 1.0-param)
excess=t[t>thresh].sum()- (t[t>thresh]-thresh).sum()? Actually t[t>thresh]=thresh, reduction sum.
t[t>thresh]=thresh
t += reduction / L
t/=t.sum()
```
This makes max <= threshold+reduction/L maybe slightly above. Good.
Add mode 'pow' flatten.
```
def spectral_square_project(a0, t_end, iters=500, mode='conv', param=0.5):
   a=normalize_sum(a0); n; L; N=next_pow2(L)
   best...
   A=np.fft.fft(a,N)  # full because target D may complex; could use full
   for it in range(iters):
      C=A*A
      c=np.fft.ifft(C).real[:L]
      c=np.maximum(c,0); csum=c.sum(); if csum<=0 break; c=c/csum
      target = make_target(c, mode, param)
      Dt=np.fft.fft(target,N)
      maxC=float(np.max(np.abs(C)))
      H=np.ones(N, dtype=complex)
      mask=np.abs(C)>1e-25*maxC
      ratio=Dt[mask]/C[mask]
      # avoid nan ratio with small |ratio|
      H[mask]=np.sqrt(ratio)
      H[~mask]=np.sqrt(Dt[~mask]/(maxC+1e-300))
      A_new=A*H
      # hermitian sym
      idx=np.arange(N)
      A_sym=0.5*(A_new + np.conj(A_new[(N-idx)%N]))
      a_new=np.fft.ifft(A_sym).real[:n]
      a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
      s=a_new.sum()
      if s<1e-12: a_new=a; break
      a_new=a_new/s
      sc=score_normalized(a_new)
      if sc<best_sc: best=a_new; best_sc=sc
      A=np.fft.fft(a_new,N)
      if time.monotonic()>t_end: break
   return best,best_sc
```
Using full FFT; N for n=10000 32768, 500 iters ~500*3 complex FFTs? ~fast. But `np.arange(N)` each iter costly; precompute idx? Pass maybe. Top-level cannot precompute per n? Inside function define idx once.
`H[~mask]` if `Dt[~mask]` zeros maybe sqrt0; okay.
Could use `np.sqrt(ratio + 0)`? If ratio negative real, sqrt imaginary; okay.

But converting ratio with branch might cause A_new magnitude not positive? okay.

- ('conv',0.2), ('conv',0.4), ('conv',0.6)
- ('pow',0.7), ('pow',0.4)
- ('clip',0.2), ('clip',0.4) where param fraction top.
Maybe too many; time. We can run from best seed 2 modes limited.

`subgrad_soft(a0, t_end, iters=1000, beta=500, step=1e-3)`:
```
def subgrad_soft(a0,t_end,iters,beta,step):
   a=normalize_sum; n; N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   x=log(a); best...
   no_accept=0
   for it:
      if time> t_end break
      A=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(A*A)[:L]; c=max(c,0)
      d=n*c; dmax=d.max(); sc=2*dmax
      update
      lower=2.0*n/(2*n-1)
      if sc-lower <1e-8: break
      sh=beta*(d-dmax); p=exp(clip)/sum
      P=rfft(p[::-1],N); conv=irfft(A*P); corr=...
      mean_grad? grad_x =4n a*(corr - dot(a,corr))
      norm = np.linalg.norm(grad_x)
      if norm<1e-18: break
      pstep=(sc-lower)/(norm*norm+1e-30)
      st=min(step, pstep, 1e-2)
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(8):
          x_new=x - st*grad_x
          a_new=softmax(x_new)
          sc_new=score_normalized(a_new)
          if sc_new + 1e-15 < sc:
              x=x_new; a=a_new; accept; break
          st*=0.5
      if accepted:
         no_accept=0; step*=1.01? maybe cap 0.05
      else:
         no_accept+=1; step*=0.7; if no_accept>100 break
   return best
```
Each iter line search up to 8 scores (FFT). Could be expensive. Use iters 200, each line search often one if step good. Good.

Need `softmax` stable and fast. If x large, `xmax=x.max(); exp(x-xmax)`. If sum inf due to overflow? subtract max prevents. If all -inf? no.

`random_seed(n, kind, rng)`:
```
def random_seed(n, kind, rng):
   i=np.arange(n)
   if kind == 'exp': a=rng.exponential(size=n)
   elif kind == 'gauss': a=np.exp(0.5*rng.standard_normal(n)) # lognormal
   elif kind == 'sine': phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); a=1+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*i/n + phase); a=np.maximum(a,0.1)
   elif kind == 'chirp': f1=rng.uniform(0.1,5); f2=rng.uniform(0.1,5); a=0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*f1*i/n + 2*np.pi*f2*i*i/(2*n) + phase); max
   elif kind == 'power': alpha=rng.uniform(0.2,2); a=1.0/(i+1)**alpha
   normalize
```
Maybe random not as good but diversity.

```
def top_k_pool(pool,k):
   idxs=list(range(len(pool)))
   # sort by score using simple selection for small pool
   out=[]
   for _ in range(min(k,len(pool))):
      if not pool: break
      minidx=0
      for i in range(1,len(pool)):
          if pool[i][0] < pool[minidx][0]: minidx=i
      out.append(pool.pop(minidx))
   return out
```

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    xm = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(xm)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
        return np.full_like(x, 1.0/len(x))
    return e/s
```
If x contains nan, return uniform? In objective if x nan, return inf.

```
def pool_add(pool, a, keep=60):
   sc=score_normalized(a)
   pool.append((sc,a))
   if len(pool)>keep:
      worst=0
      for i in range(1,len(pool)):
         if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]: worst=i
      del pool[worst]
```
If sc inf, skip.

`get_top(pool,k)`:
```
def get_top(pool,k):
    res=[]
    used=[False]*len(pool)
    for _ in range(min(k,len(pool))):
       best=-1; bestsc=1e300
       for i in range(len(pool)):
          if not used[i] and pool[i][0] < bestsc:
             bestsc=pool[i][0]; best=i
       if best<0: break
       used[best]=True; res.append(pool[best])
    return res
```

```
   # But if hidden uses sum maybe? score scale invariant unless clipping; sum1 safe.
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=min(2400.0,float(budget_s))
    reserve=90.0
    opt_end=t0+max(10,total-reserve)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pool=[]
    # parent
    parent=None
    try:

           p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float).ravel()
           if p.size and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):

              if parent.sum()>1e-15:
                 parent=parent/parent.sum()

       add_candidate(pool,a)
    # Subgrad polish best until 90%

    # Final best internal

    if best_a is None: best_a=np.ones(base_n)/base_n; best_sc=score_normalized(best_a)
    # final eval
    final_candidates=[]
    final_candidates.append(best_a)
    if parent is not None: final_candidates.append(parent)
    final_candidates.append(np.ones(base_n)/base_n)
    # add top pool
    top_final=get_top(pool,6)

    # deduplicate by pointer? We can just limit and avoid too many same. Use a set of id(array) not possible? Could use hash of length and first value? Simpler evaluate max 10 unique by `id` not reliable after copies. We'll just evaluate sequential and stop if too many.

    t_eval_end=t0+total-15
    for a in final_candidates:
       if a is None or len(a)==0: continue
       if time.monotonic() > t_eval_end: break

       val=safe_evaluate(out)
       if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_actual_score:
          best_actual_score=val; best_actual_a=out
       if len(evaluated)>8: break
    # verify final best_actual_a
    final_val=safe_evaluate(best_actual_a)
    if not finite: fallback uniform eval
    print
    return best_actual_a
```

```
def run_lbfgs_soft(a0, betas, t_end, maxfun_base=2000):
    a=normalize_sum(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1: return a,2.0
    best_a=a.copy(); best=sc=score_normalized(a)
    x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); x-=x.max()
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now > t_end - 5: break
       maxfun=max(200, int(maxfun_base*(1.0+0.1*idx)))
       maxiter=max(100, int(maxfun*0.8))
       print(f"  LSE beta={beta:.0f} n={n} maxfun={maxfun}")
       try:
          res=minimize(lse_soft_obj, x, args=(beta,n,N), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={...})
          x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
          a=softmax(x)
          sc2=score_normalized(a)
          if sc2 < best_sc:
              best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc2
              print(f"    improved {best_sc:.8f}")
          x=x-x.max()
       except Exception as e:
          print("    lbfgs error", e)
    return best_a,best_sc
```
`maxfun` and `maxiter` might be too low; but okay. If `t_end` far, each beta maybe 2000 evals; 8*2000=16000. Time maybe. Set `maxfun_base=1500` in main call to be safe. We can adjust.

```
def subgrad_soft(a0,t_end,iters,beta,step):
   a=normalize_sum; n; N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   x=np.log...; best...
   no=0
   for it in range(iters):
      now=time.monotonic(); if now>t_end-2: break
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]; c=max(c,0)
      d=n*c; dmax=float(d.max()); sc=2.0*dmax
      if sc<best_sc: update
      lower=2.0*n/(2*n-1)
      if sc-lower<1e-8: break
      sh=beta*(d-dmax); es=exp(clip); p=es/sum
      P=...corr...
      mean_grad=4n dot(a,corr)
      grad=4n a*(corr - dot(a,corr))
      norm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
      if norm<1e-16: break
      pstep=(sc-lower)/(norm*norm+1e-30)
      st=min(step,pstep,5e-3)
      if st<1e-12: break
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(6):
          x_new=x-st*grad
          a_new=softmax(x_new)
          sc_new=score_normalized(a_new)
          if sc_new + 1e-15 < sc:
              x=x_new; a=a_new; accepted=True; break
          st*=0.5
      if accepted:
          no=0
          step=min(0.02, step*1.05)
      else:
          no+=1; step*=0.6
          if no>50: break
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def make_output(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return [0.5]*64
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01:
      a=np.ones(64)*0.5
   else:
      a=a/s
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```

```
def make_target(c, mode, param):
    L = c.size
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    c = c / s
    if mode == 'conv':
        alpha = float(np.clip(param, 0.0, 1.0))
        t = (1.0 - alpha)*c + alpha*(1.0/L)
    elif mode == 'pow':
        gamma = float(np.clip(param, 0.05, 2.0))
        t = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), gamma)
        ts = t.sum()
        if ts <= 0: t = c
        else: t = t/ts
    elif mode == 'clip':
        # param as fraction of upper tail to flatten, e.g. 0.2
        q = 1.0 - float(np.clip(param, 0.01, 0.95))
        thresh = float(np.quantile(c, q))
        t = c.copy()
        above = t > thresh
        reduction = float((t[above] - thresh).sum())
        t[above] = thresh
        t = t + reduction / L
        ts=t.sum()
        if ts>0: t=t/ts
    else:
        t=c
    return t
```
No lambda.

In spectral, using `mode='pow', param=0.5` t=c^0.5 flatten. Good.

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2 over non-negative a.

Strategy:
- Use the strong known binomial starting point a_k = C(2k,k)/4^k, plus
  reversed/tapered/mixed variants.
- Directly optimize in log-softmax variables using an LSE smoothing of the
  peak coefficient. The gradient is computed via FFT correlations.
- Use a Fourier-domain square-root projection to push conv(a,a) toward a flatter
  profile (the square counterpart of Fienup lifting).
- Polish with a subgradient descent step targeting near-active lags.
- Keep a pool of candidates and verify/return the best actual evaluated sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

BETAS_MAIN = (10.0, 25.0, 60.0, 150.0, 400.0, 1000.0, 2500.0, 6000.0)
BETAS_SHORT = (30.0, 100.0, 400.0, 1500.0)


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return int(1) << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / float(x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    xm = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(xm)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / float(x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def linear_conv(a, N=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    return np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)


def score_normalized(a):
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0
    c = linear_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx


def lse_soft_obj(x, beta, n, N):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax(x)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    # Work with d = n*c so numerical scale is O(1). Exact score is 2*max(d).
    d = float(n) * c
    dmax = float(d.max())

    shifted = beta * (d - dmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -500.0, 500.0)
    es = np.exp(shifted)
    st = float(es.sum())
    if st <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(st):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    p = es / st
    lse = dmax + float(np.log(st) / beta)

    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)

    if n == 1:
        corr = np.array([float(a[0] * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        corr = conv[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1].astype(np.float64, copy=True)

    mean_corr = float(np.dot(a, corr))
    # grad = 4*n*a*(corr - mean(corr under a))
    g = 4.0 * float(n) * (corr - mean_corr)
    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, g))
    grad_x = a * (g - mean_g)

    reg = 1e-14
    val = 2.0 * lse + reg * float(np.dot(x, x))
    grad_x = grad_x + 2.0 * reg * x

    if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad_x


def run_lbfgs_soft(a0, betas, t_end, maxfun_base=1500):
    a = normalize_sum(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 2.0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(a)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.max(x))
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if not np.isfinite(t_end) or now > t_end - 8.0:
            break

        maxfun = max(250, int(maxfun_base * (1.0 + 0.15 * float(idx))))
        maxiter = max(100, int(0.8 * maxfun))
        print(f"    LSE-beta={float(beta):9.4f} n={n} maxfun~{maxfun}")

        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_soft_obj,
                x,
                args=(float(beta), int(n), int(N)),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-17,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxls": 50,
                },
            )
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            a = softmax(x)
            sc = score_normalized(a)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                print(f"      improved -> {best_sc:.8f}")
            x = x - float(np.max(x))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a, best_sc


def make_target(c, mode, param):
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    c = c / s

    if mode == "conv":
        alpha = float(np.clip(param, 0.0, 1.0))
        t = (1.0 - alpha) * c + alpha * (1.0 / float(L))
    elif mode == "pow":
        gamma = float(np.clip(param, 0.05, 2.0))
        t = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), gamma)
        ts = float(t.sum())
        if ts <= 1e-300:
            t = c
        else:
            t = t / ts
    elif mode == "clip":
        # Flatten the upper `param` tail and redistribute excess uniformly.
        q = 1.0 - float(np.clip(param, 0.01, 0.95))
        thresh = float(np.quantile(c, q))
        t = c.copy()
        above = t > thresh
        reduction = float((t[above] - thresh).sum())
        t[above] = thresh
        t = t + reduction / float(L)
        ts = float(t.sum())
        if ts > 1e-300:
            t = t / ts
    else:
        t = c
    return np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)


def spectral_square_project(a0, t_end, iters=300, mode="conv", param=0.5):
    a = normalize_sum(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 2.0

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(a)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)

    idx = np.arange(N)
    rev_idx = (N - idx) % N

    iters = int(max(1, iters))
    for _ in range(iters):
        if not np.isfinite(t_end) or time.monotonic() > t_end - 2.0:
            break

        C = A * A
        c = np.fft.ifft(C).real[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        cs = float(c.sum())
        if cs <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cs):
            break
        c = c / cs

        target = make_target(c, mode, param)
        Dt = np.fft.fft(target, N)

        maxC = float(np.max(np.abs(C)))
        if maxC <= 1e-300:
            break

        H = np.ones(N, dtype=complex)
        mask = np.abs(C) > 1e-25 * maxC
        ratio = Dt[mask] / C[mask]
        H[mask] = np.sqrt(ratio)
        H[~mask] = np.sqrt(Dt[~mask] / (maxC + 1e-300))

        A_new = A * H
        A_sym = 0.5 * (A_new + np.conj(A_new[rev_idx]))
        a_new = np.fft.ifft(A_sym).real[:n]
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        s = float(a_new.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            break
        a_new = a_new / s

        sc = score_normalized(a_new)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            best_sc = sc

        A = np.fft.fft(a_new, N)

    return best_a, best_sc


def subgrad_soft(a0, t_end, iters=500, beta=1000.0, step=0.01):
    a = normalize_sum(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 2.0

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(a)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.max(x))

    iters = int(max(1, iters))
    step = float(step)
    no_accept = 0

    for _ in range(iters):
        if not np.isfinite(t_end) or time.monotonic() > t_end - 3.0:
            break

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        d = float(n) * c
        dmax = float(d.max())
        sc = 2.0 * dmax
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        lower = 2.0 * float(n) / float(2 * n - 1)
        if sc - lower < 1e-8:
            break

        shifted = beta * (d - dmax)
        shifted = np.clip(shifted, -500.0, 500.0)
        es = np.exp(shifted)
        st = float(es.sum())
        if st <= 1e-300:
            break
        p = es / st

        P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
        if n == 1:
            corr = np.array([float(a[0] * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            corr = conv[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1].astype(np.float64, copy=True)

        dot = float(np.dot(a, corr))
        g = 4.0 * float(n) * (corr - dot)
        mean_g = float(np.dot(a, g))
        grad_x = a * (g - mean_g)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad_x))
        if norm <= 1e-16:
            break

        pstep = (sc - lower) / (norm * norm + 1e-30)
        st = min(step, pstep, 5e-3)
        if st <= 1e-13:
            break

        accepted = False
        for _ls in range(6):
            x_new = x - st * grad_x
            a_new = softmax(x_new)
            sc_new = score_normalized(a_new)
            if sc_new + 1e-15 < sc:
                x = x_new
                a = a_new
                accepted = True
                break
            st *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            step = min(0.02, step * 1.05)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            step *= 0.6
            if no_accept > 80:
                break

    return best_a, best_sc


def binomial_unnorm(n):
    a = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    val = 1.0
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, int(n)):
        val *= (float(k) - 0.5) / (2.0 * float(k))
        a[k] = val
    return a


def binomial_sequence(n):
    return normalize_sum(binomial_unnorm(n))


def tapered_binomial(n, alpha, power):
    b = binomial_unnorm(int(n))
    i = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if int(n) > 0:
        t = i / float(int(n))
    else:
        t = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    mask = 1.0 - float(alpha) * np.power(t, float(power))
    mask = np.maximum(mask, 1e-6)
    return normalize_sum(b * mask)


def random_seed(n, kind, rng):
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == "exp":
        a = rng.exponential(size=n)
    elif kind == "lognormal":
        a = np.exp(0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif kind == "sine":
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * i / float(n) + phase)
    elif kind == "chirp":
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 6.0))
        f2 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 8.0))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(
            2.0 * np.pi * f1 * i / float(n)
            + np.pi * f2 * i * i / float(n)
            + phase
        )
    else:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5))
        a = 1.0 / np.power(i + 1.0, alpha)
    return normalize_sum(np.maximum(a, 1e-6))


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    old_n = int(a.size)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    return np.asarray(np.interp(x_new, x_old, a), dtype=np.float64)


def add_candidate(pool, a, keep=80):
    if a is None:
        return
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    sc = score_normalized(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    pool.append((sc, a))
    if len(pool) > keep:
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        del pool[worst]


def best_in_pool(pool):
    if not pool:
        return None, float("inf")
    best_i = 0
    for i in range(1, len(pool)):
        if pool[i][0] < pool[best_i][0]:
            best_i = i
    return pool[best_i][1], pool[best_i][0]


def get_top(pool, k):
    if not pool or k <= 0:
        return []
    used = [False] * len(pool)
    out = []
    for _ in range(min(int(k), len(pool))):
        best_i = -1
        best_sc = float("inf")
        for i in range(len(pool)):
            if (not used[i]) and pool[i][0] < best_sc:
                best_sc = pool[i][0]
                best_i = i
        if best_i < 0:
            break
        used[best_i] = True
        out.append(pool[best_i])
    return out


def safe_evaluate(seq):
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        return val
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def make_output(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return [0.5] * 64
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        a = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = min(float(budget_s), 2400.0)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    total_budget = max(total_budget, 120.0)
    opt_end = t0 + max(30.0, total_budget - 90.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pool = []

    # Load candidate from previous run if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals() and height_sequence_1 is not None:
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                pc = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                if float(pc.sum()) > 1e-15:
                    parent = pc / float(pc.sum())
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate(pool, parent)
        print(f"Parent loaded: n={parent.size}, internal_score={score_normalized(parent):.8f}")

    base_n = 10000
    if parent is not None and 512 <= parent.size <= 25000:
        base_n = int(parent.size)
    base_n = int(max(512, min(base_n, 20000)))

    # Core structured seeds.
    b = binomial_sequence(base_n)
    br = b[::-1]
    bp = 0.5 * (b + br)
    add_candidate(pool, b, 100)
    add_candidate(pool, br, 100)
    add_candidate(pool, bp, 100)

    # Mix with uniform to move away from the pure left-spiked binomial.
    for mix in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
        u = np.full(base_n, 1.0 / float(base_n), dtype=np.float64)
        add_candidate(pool, float(mix) * b + (1.0 - float(mix)) * u, 100)

    # Tapered binomial variants.
    for alpha in (-0.2, -0.1, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4):
        for pw in (0.5, 1.0):
            add_candidate(pool, tapered_binomial(base_n, alpha, pw), 100)

    # Random diversity.
    for kind in ("exp", "lognormal", "sine", "chirp"):
        add_candidate(pool, random_seed(base_n, kind, rng), 100)

    # Choose optimization starts.
    starts = get_top(pool, 5)
    # Always consider the palindromic mix if not already among top starts.
    adds_special = False
    for _, a in starts:
        if a.size == bp.size and np.linalg.norm(a - bp) <= 1e-12:
            adds_special = True
            break
    if not adds_special:
        starts.append((score_normalized(bp), bp))
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append((score_normalized(parent), parent))
    starts = get_top(starts, 6)

    main_seed = starts[0][1] if starts else b
    main_seed = normalize_sum(main_seed)

    print(f"\n=== Phase 1: spectral projection on main seed (n={base_n}) ===")
    phase1_end = t0 + 0.10 * max(1.0, opt_end - t0)
    for mode, prm in (("conv", 0.4), ("pow", 0.5)):
        if time.monotonic() < phase1_end:
            a, sc = spectral_square_project(main_seed, phase1_end, 350, mode, prm)
            add_candidate(pool, a, 100)
            if sc < score_normalized(main_seed):
                main_seed = normalize_sum(a)

    main_a, main_sc = best_in_pool(pool)
    if main_a is None:
        main_a, main_sc = main_seed, score_normalized(main_seed)
    print(f"Best after spectral phase: {main_sc:.8f}, n={len(main_a)}")

    print("\n=== Phase 2: main LSE annealing ===")
    main_exec_end = t0 + 0.58 * max(1.0, opt_end - t0)
    if time.monotonic() < main_exec_end:
        a, sc = run_lbfgs_soft(main_a, BETAS_MAIN, main_exec_end, maxfun_base=1800)
        add_candidate(pool, a, 100)
        main_a, main_sc = best_in_pool(pool)
        print(f"Best after main LSE: {main_sc:.8f}, n={len(main_a)}")

    print("\n=== Phase 3: secondary starts ===")
    sec_end = t0 + 0.80 * max(1.0, opt_end - t0)
    for i in range(1, min(5, len(starts))):
        if time.monotonic() > sec_end:
            break
        sc_seed, a_seed = starts[i]
        if sc_seed > main_sc + 0.25:
            continue
        a_seed = normalize_sum(a_seed)
        a1, _ = spectral_square_project(a_seed, min(sec_end, time.monotonic() + 70.0), 180, "conv", 0.5)
        add_candidate(pool, a1, 120)
        a2, _ = run_lbfgs_soft(a1, BETAS_SHORT, sec_end, maxfun_base=800)
        add_candidate(pool, a2, 120)

    print("\n=== Phase 4: subgradient + final LSE polish ===")
    polish_end = t0 + 0.92 * max(1.0, opt_end - t0)
    main_a, main_sc = best_in_pool(pool)
    if main_a is not None and time.monotonic() < polish_end:
        a, sc = subgrad_soft(main_a, polish_end, 900, beta=1500.0, step=0.01)
        add_candidate(pool, a, 120)
        main_a, main_sc = best_in_pool(pool)

    if time.monotonic() < opt_end - 25.0:
        a, sc = run_lbfgs_soft(main_a, (5000.0, 15000.0), opt_end, maxfun_base=1400)
        add_candidate(pool, a, 120)

    print("\n=== Phase 5: larger-n candidate ===")
    if time.monotonic() < opt_end - 70.0:
        big_a, big_sc = best_in_pool(pool)
        if big_a is not None and big_a.size < 18000:
            new_n = int(min(18000, max(12000, big_a.size + 4096)))
            a_interp = interpolate_sequence(big_a, new_n)
            add_candidate(pool, a_interp, 120)
            a_bin = binomial_sequence(new_n)
            add_candidate(pool, a_bin, 120)
            if time.monotonic() < opt_end - 40.0:
                a_sp, _ = spectral_square_project(a_interp, opt_end, 220, "conv", 0.4)
                add_candidate(pool, a_sp, 120)
            if time.monotonic() < opt_end - 25.0:
                a_lse, _ = run_lbfgs_soft(a_sp, BETAS_SHORT, opt_end, maxfun_base=700)
                add_candidate(pool, a_lse, 120)

    best_a, best_sc = best_in_pool(pool)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.full(base_n, 1.0 / float(base_n), dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = score_normalized(best_a)
    print(f"\nBest internal overall: {best_sc:.8f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # Final candidates for actual evaluator.
    final_candidates = []
    final_candidates.append(best_a)
    if parent is not None:
        final_candidates.append(parent)
    final_candidates.append(np.full(base_n, 1.0 / float(base_n), dtype=np.float64))
    for _, a in get_top(pool, 8):
        final_candidates.append(a)

    best_actual_score = float("inf")
    best_actual_out = make_output(best_a)
    final_eval_end = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - 20.0)
    tried = 0

    for a in final_candidates:
        if a is None or a.size == 0 or tried >= 10:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > final_eval_end:
            break
        out = make_output(a)
        val = safe_evaluate(out)
        tried += 1
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_actual_score:
            best_actual_score = val
            best_actual_out = out
            print(f"  actual candidate evaluated: {val:.8f} (n={len(out)})")

    final_val = safe_evaluate(best_actual_out)
    if not np.isfinite(final_val):
        fallback = np.full(10000, 1.0 / 10000.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_actual_out = make_output(fallback)
        final_val = safe_evaluate(best_actual_out)

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL: internal_best={best_sc:.8f}, verified={final_val:.8f}")
    print(f"Length={len(best_actual_out)}, elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s / budget={total_budget:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return best_actual_out
```
